&lt;project_context>
# 项目: provenance_weight_training

## 运行环境
- 服务器名: westd-30476
- SSH 账户: root（调用 ssh_execute / submit_training_job 时传入 account="root"）
- SSH 连接命令: ssh connect.westd.seetacloud.com  # 必须用此命令，~/.ssh/config 自动应用密钥和跳板机，禁止用裸 IP
- 项目代码根目录: /root/provenance_weight_training


## 环境搭建
连接服务器后先调 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果匹配，跳过基础环境搭建，只装项目特有依赖。已预装的包不要重复安装。

## 输出风格
回复简洁直接。只输出执行结果和必要信息，不要解释过程、不要客套、不要给未被要求的建议。
&lt;/project_context>
OOD 数据文件在 westd-30476 上不存在，需要从 westd-41312 同步过去，然后跑 OOD eval。

**第一步：确认 westd-41312 上 OOD 数据路径**

ssh_read_only 到 westd-41312：
```bash
find /root/provenance_weight_training/data -name "*openwebtext*" -o -name "*owt*" -o -name "*wikipedia*" 2>/dev/null
find /root/autodl-tmp/provenance_weight_training/data -name "*openwebtext*" -o -name "*owt*" -o -name "*wikipedia*" 2>/dev/null
ls -la /root/provenance_weight_training/data/ood_openwebtext.jsonl /root/provenance_weight_training/data/ood_wikipedia.jsonl 2>/dev/null
ls -la /root/provenance_weight_training/data/openwebtext*.jsonl /root/provenance_weight_training/data/wikipedia*.jsonl 2>/dev/null
```

记录完整路径和文件大小。

**第二步：传输到 westd-30476**

从 westd-41312 上用 scp 传输到 westd-30476。先确认两台服务器能互通：

在 westd-41312 上执行（ssh_execute）：
```bash
scp /root/provenance_weight_training/data/&lt;OOD_FILE_1> root@westd-30476:/root/provenance_weight_training/data/
scp /root/provenance_weight_training/data/&lt;OOD_FILE_2> root@westd-30476:/root/provenance_weight_training/data/
```

如果 scp 不通（网络隔离），改用其他方案：
- 在 westd-30476 上用 python streaming 重新下载（eval_ppl_ood.py 本身可能支持 streaming）
- 或用 huggingface datasets 库直接 stream

**第三步：验证数据到位**

ssh_read_only 到 westd-30476：
```bash
ls -la /root/provenance_weight_training/data/*openwebtext* /root/provenance_weight_training/data/*wikipedia* 2>/dev/null
wc -l /root/provenance_weight_training/data/*openwebtext* /root/provenance_weight_training/data/*wikipedia* 2>/dev/null
```

确认文件存在且非空。

**第四步：在 westd-30476 上跑 OOD eval**

确认 eval_ppl_ood.py 存在：
```bash
ls /root/provenance_weight_training/eval_ppl_ood.py
python /root/provenance_weight_training/eval_ppl_ood.py --help 2>&1 | head -20
```

然后在 4 个 GPU 上并行启动 8 个 checkpoint 的 OOD eval（每 GPU 串行 2 个）：

环境: `source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`
路径: `cd /root/provenance_weight_training`
CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES 必须显式设置。

**cuda:0**: epoch_matched_dr_seed123 → epoch_matched_dr_seed456
**cuda:1**: detect_remove_q02_seed42 → detect_remove_q05_seed123
**cuda:2**: detect_remove_q05_seed456 → detect_remove_q03_seed123
**cuda:3**: detect_remove_q03_seed456 → detect_remove_q01_seed42

Checkpoint 路径（全在 `/root/autodl-tmp/output/models/` 下）：
- epoch_matched_dr_seed123/final
- epoch_matched_dr_seed456/final
- detect_remove_q02_seed42/final
- detect_remove_q05_seed123/final
- detect_remove_q05_seed456/final
- detect_remove_q03_seed123/final
- detect_remove_q03_seed456/final
- detect_remove_q01_seed42/final

用 nohup 并行启动：
```bash
nohup bash -c "source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cd /root/provenance_weight_training && CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 python eval_ppl_ood.py --checkpoint_path /root/autodl-tmp/output/models/epoch_matched_dr_seed123/final --max_docs 5000 --stride 1024 --fp16 && CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 python eval_ppl_ood.py --checkpoint_path /root/autodl-tmp/output/models/epoch_matched_dr_seed456/final --max_docs 5000 --stride 1024 --fp16" > /tmp/ood_gpu0.log 2>&1 &
```
（类似命令对 cuda:1/2/3）

**第五步：轮询收集结果**

每个 eval ~2-5 分钟。轮询日志直到完成。

结果格式应包含: eval_ppl_openwebtext, eval_ppl_wikipedia

**第六步：写入 registry**

用 update_registry 将 OOD 指标 merge 到每个实验的 metrics（不覆盖已有 holdout PPL）。

对应 registry exp_id（检查 registry 中实际存在的 exp_id）：
- epoch_matched_dr_seed123 → 可能是这个名字，或 eval_epoch_matched_seed123
- detect_remove_q02_seed42
- detect_remove_q05_seed123 / detect_remove_q05_seed456
- detect_remove_q03_seed123 / detect_remove_q03_seed456
- detect_remove_q01_seed42

**第七步：验证结果**

每个 eval 完成后验证结果非空、PPL 值合理（应在 15-20 范围内）。

**最终汇总**：
| exp_id | OWT PPL | Wiki PPL | 状态 |
缺失或失败的标注原因。

- `detect_remove_q03_epoch_matched_seed42` — DONE, PPL=17.74, success. Key interpretation: "Under fixed training budget, stricter filtering (q03) actually hurts slightly vs moderate filtering (q05). The PPL advantage of q03 in multi-epoch setting comes from more epochs, not higher per-token quality."
- `eval_q03_r2` — DONE, PPL=17.25, success
- `threshold_q03_seed42` — DONE, PPL=17.25, success

**Q04:**

**Q05:**

- `detect_remove_q05_epoch_matched_seed42` — DONE, PPL=17.65, success. Key interpretation: "D&R 0.36 PPL advantage over epoch-matched is entirely from multi-epoch training on clean data, not from data quality filtering."
- `detect_remove_q05_seed123` — DONE, PPL=17.27, success
- `detect_remove_q05_seed456` — DONE, PPL=17.28, success

**Q06:**

**Summary of key patterns:**
- Multi-epoch D&R runs (q02-q05) achieve best PPL around 17.25-17.28, matching or beating the baseline (17.29)
- Epoch-matched experiments consistently land around PPL 17.62-17.74, much worse than their multi-epoch counterparts
- This confirms the conclusion recorded in the metrics: the PPL gains from detect-and-remove come primarily from multi-epoch training on filtered data, not from per-token data quality improvements

        "text": "Here are the 29 matching experiments (out of 192 total), grouped by category:\n\n---\n\n**Baseline:**\n- `detect_remove_baseline_seed42` — DONE, PPL=17.29, success\n\n**Q01 (strictest filtering):**\n- `detect_remove_q01_seed42` — DONE, PPL=17.40, success\n- `detect_remove_q01_epoch_matched_seed42` — DONE, PPL=18.04, success\n- `eval_q01_final` — PREPARING, no metrics\n- `eval_q01_final_v2` — DONE, PPL=17.40, success\n\n**Q02:**\n- `detect_remove_q02_seed42` — DONE, PPL=17.27, success\n\n**Q03:**\n- `detect_remove_q03_seed42` — DONE, no metrics, **failed**\n- `detect_remove_q03_seed42_r2` — DONE, PPL=17.25, success\n- `detect_remove_q03_seed123` — DONE, PPL=17.25, success\n- `detect_remove_q03_seed456` — DONE, PPL=17.25, success\n- `detect_remove_q03_epoch_matched_seed42` — DONE, PPL=17.74, success. Key interpretation: \"Under fixed training budget, stricter filtering (q03) actually hurts slightly vs moderate filtering (q05). The PPL advantage of q03 in multi-epoch setting comes from more epochs, not higher per-token quality.\"\n- `eval_q03_r2` — DONE, PPL=17.25, success\n- `threshold_q03_seed42` — DONE, PPL=17.25, success\n\n**Q04:**\n- `detect_remove_q04_seed42` — DONE, PPL=17.26, success\n\n**Q05:**\n- `detect_remove_q05_epoch_matched` — PREPARING, no metrics\n- `detect_remove_q05_epoch_matched_seed42` — DONE, PPL=17.65, success. Key interpretation: \"D&R 0.36 PPL advantage over epoch-matched is entirely from multi-epoch training on clean data, not from data quality filtering.\"\n- `detect_remove_q05_seed123` — DONE, PPL=17.27, success\n- `detect_remove_q05_seed456` — DONE, PPL=17.28, success\n\n**Q06:**\n- `detect_remove_q06_seed42` — DONE, PPL=17.30, success\n\n**Q07:**\n- `detect_remove_q07_seed42` — DONE, PPL=17.32, success\n- `detect_remove_q07_seed42_r2` — PREPARING, no metrics\n- `detect_remove_q07_epoch_matched_seed42` — DONE, PPL=17.62, success\n\n**Q08:**\n- `detect_remove_q08_seed42` — DONE, PPL=17.34, success\n\n**Q09:**\n- `detect_remove_q09_seed42` — DONE, PPL=17.42, success\n- `detect_remove_q09_epoch_matched_seed42` — DONE, PPL=17.63, success\n\n**Epoch-matched (seed variations):**\n- `epoch_matched_dr_seed123` — DONE, PPL=17.66, success\n- `epoch_matched_dr_seed456` — DONE, PPL=17.66, success\n- `eval_epoch_matched_seed123` — DONE, PPL=17.66, success\n- `eval_epoch_matched_seed456` — DONE, PPL=17.66, success\n\n---\n\n**Summary of key patterns:**\n- Multi-epoch D&R runs (q02-q05) achieve best PPL around 17.25-17.28, matching or beating the baseline (17.29)\n- Epoch-matched experiments consistently land around PPL 17.62-17.74, much worse than their multi-epoch counterparts\n- This confirms the conclusion recorded in the metrics: the PPL gains from detect-and-remove come primarily from multi-epoch training on filtered data, not from per-token data quality improvements\n- 3 experiments are still in PREPARING status; 1 (q03_seed42) failed with no metrics"
      }

{
  "server": "westd-30476",
  "command": "nohup bash -c 'source /etc/network_turbo && source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && export HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache && export HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET=1 && export REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt && export SSL_CERT_FILE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt && export HF_HUB_DOWNLOAD_TIMEOUT=300 && python3 -c \"\nimport os, json, time\nos.environ[\\\"HF_HUB_DOWNLOAD_TIMEOUT\\\"] = \\\"300\\\"\nos.environ[\\\"HF_DATASETS_DOWNLOAD_TIMEOUT\\\"] = \\\"300\\\"\nfrom huggingface_hub import configure_http_backend\nimport requests\ndef backend_factory():\n    session = requests.Session()\n    session.timeout = 300\n    return session\nconfigure_http_backend(backend_factory=backend_factory)\nfrom datasets import load_dataset\nprint(\\\"Starting wikipedia download...\\\", flush=True)\nt0 = time.time()\nds = load_dataset(\\\"wikimedia/wikipedia\\\", \\\"20231101.en\\\", split=\\\"train\\\", streaming=True, cache_dir=\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\\\")\ntexts = []\nout_path = \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/ood_wikipedia_5000.jsonl\\\"\nwith open(out_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\n    for i, example in enumerate(ds):\n        if len(texts) >= 5000:\n            break\n        t = example[\\\"text\\\"]\n        if len(t.strip()) < 50:\n            continue\n        texts.append(t)\n        f.write(json.dumps({\\\"text\\\": t}) + chr(10))\n        if len(texts) % 1000 == 0:\n            print(f\\\"  {len(texts)} docs downloaded ({time.time()-t0:.0f}s)\\\", flush=True)\nprint(f\\\"Done: {len(texts)} docs in {time.time()-t0:.0f}s\\\")\n\"' > /tmp/wiki_download3.log 2>&1 &\necho $!",
  "account": "root"
}
